If [[Necron]] is the right-hand of the [[The Wither King|Wither King]], this dark demigod is the left-hand.
— [[Maddox the Slayer|Maddox]] the Slayer
The Voidgloom Seraph (/ˈsɛrəf/, "the burning one"; plural Seraphim /ˈsɛrəfɪm/) is a [[Slayer]] [[Bosses|Boss]] in the [[Enderman Slayer]] branch. It is an Enderman with strong attacks. It is significantly stronger than the previous Slayer bosses.
Defeating the Tier III Voidgloom Seraph results in unlocking the [[Blaze Slayer]] quest and the [[Inferno Demonlord]] boss.

## Behavior
The Voidgloom Seraph is an Enderman-type mob hostile to all players. It has the appearance of an enderman with critical particles following it. It has a smaller aggro range compared to other Slayers.
[[Aspect of the Dragons]] and [[Ice Spray Wand]] don't affect Voidgloom Seraph. They are immune to water damage. Like normal Endermen, they are also immune to the damage from most arrows, except those shot using a [[Juju Shortbow]] or [[Terminator]]. However, regardless of the bow type, they can all apply 1 hit per arrow.
When the Voidgloom Seraphim's Hitshield is not active, [[Ferocity]] that is effective on them is divided by 4.
As with all Slayer bosses, if the Voidgloom Seraph has not dealt damage to a player for some time, it will begin rapidly regenerating [[Health]]. The regeneration immediately stops when it hits a player again. If 4 minutes pass without the boss being defeated, it will despawn, failing the Slayer quest. A timer is shown above its head indicating the remaining time.
[[Voidling Fanatic]] and [[Voidling Extremist]] spawns are suppressed in the vicinity of a Voidgloom Seraph.
Each tier has all of the abilities from the ones before it.
|Tier|[[Health]]|[[Damage]]|Ability|Slayer XP Reward|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|I|300k [[Health]]|800|Dissonance Once in a while, teleport behind or to the sides of the player. Also, the boss deals half of its DPS as AOE.|5 XP|
|I|300k [[Health]]|800|Malevolent Hitshield Gain a shield for 15 (Tier I)/30 (Tier II)/60 (Tier III)/100 (Tier IV) hits, reduced on hit regardless of damage. Receives -75% [[Ferocity]] from your hits while the shield is NOT active. The boss gains 2000 DPS when the shield is up (Tier III and IV).|5 XP|
|II|12M [[Health]]|2,720|Yang Glyphs The boss holds a beacon for 2 seconds then throws it to a random spot nearby. Touch the beacon with your body to break the glyph. Failing to break a glyph within 5s instantly kills you.|25 XP|
|III|50M [[Health]]|7,650|Nukekubi Fixations Spawns heads which will travel to a random location around the player and then float there. Clear heads by looking at them for half a second. Each head deals 600/1500 DPS. That amount is doubled for each other head alive.|100 XP|
|IV|210M [[Health]]|11,000|Broken Heart Radiation Becomes immune to damage and casts dangerous moving beams. Touching a beam deals 800 [[True Damage]]. Touching a beam also decreases your [[Vitality]] by 12 for 90s.|500 XP|
### Tier I
- Malevolent Hitshield appears upon spawning and reaching 200k [[Health]] (2/3) and 100k [[Health]] (1/3). The boss does not gain DPS while the shield is up. The shield has no invincibility frames. All types of arrows can remove 1 hit per shot from the shield. It has 15 hits. Purple particles surround the Seraph when its shield is up. When attacked in this state, it teleports into a block within a 1 block radius by up to 60 times per second. Upon the shield being destroyed, the boss resumes attacking as normal.
- Dissonance begin when the boss is alive. The boss teleports behind or next to the player who spawned it, which happens more often if the player doesn't move. It deals 40% of its DPS to all players fighting it in a 6.5 block radius. However, in and only in Tier I bosses, the AOE damage doesn't apply. The teleportation, however, remains usable for all tiers of bosses.
### Tier II
- Malevolent Hitshield appears upon spawning and reaching 8M [[Health]] (2/3) and 4M [[Health]] (1/3). It has 30 hits. The boss gains +2,000 DPS every 3s the shield is up; this bonus DPS is removed once the shield is destroyed.
- Yang Glyphs appear upon reaching 6M [[Health]] (1/2).
  - The Seraph will begin to hold Yang Glyphs, which appear as a beacon. After 2 seconds, it will throw the glyph to a nearby spot. Any player fighting the Seraph can destroy the glyph by standing adjacent to or on top of it. If it is not destroyed within 5 seconds, it deals 1000 × Max [[Health]] in [[True Damage]] to all players fighting the Seraph. The abilities of the [[Mithril Coat]] and [[Wither Cloak Sword]] can block this damage. Glyphs cannot be thrown when the Hitshield is up. The Seraph will continue to try to throw Glyphs until it is defeated.
### Tier III
- Malevolent Hitshield appears upon spawning and reaching 33,333,333 [[Health]] (2/3) and 16,666,666 [[Health]] (1/3). It has 60 hits. The boss gains +2,000 DPS every 3s the shield is up; this bonus DPS is removed once the shield is destroyed.
- Yang Glyphs begin at 33,333,333 [[Health]] (2/3) and are thrown more often.
- Nukekubi Fixation heads spawn around the player it is attacking upon reaching 16,666,666 [[Health]] (1/3). The heads can be destroyed by looking directly at them for 0.5s. They can be destroyed through walls. When they are destroyed, they will play a hissing sound. Each head attacks with a faint blue laser, and increases the Dissonance damage by 600 per second, which is doubled for each head alive. If the heads' damage is more than the boss's base damage, the boss's damage is nullified and only heads deal damage.
The Nukekubi Fixation
DPS by Active Heads (Tier III):
|Number of heads|Total damage per second|
|-|-|
|1|600|

|5|48,000|
### Tier IV
- Malevolent Hitshield appears upon spawning and reaching 140M [[Health]] (2/3) and 70M [[Health]] (1/3). It has 100 hits. The boss gains +2,000 DPS every 3s the shield is up; this bonus DPS is removed once the shield is destroyed.
- Yang Glyphs begin at 140M [[Health]] (2/3) and are thrown at the same rate as Tier III.
- Nukekubi Fixation heads spawn upon reaching 70M [[Health]] (1/3). Each head deals base 1,500 DPS, which is doubled for each head alive.
DPS by Active Heads (Tier IV):
|Number of heads|Total damage per second|
|-|-|
|1|1,500|
|2|6,000|
|3|18,000|
|4|48,000|
|5|120,000|
- Broken Heart Radiation occurs at 175M [[Health]] (5/6), 105M [[Health]] (1/2) and 35M [[Health]] (1/6).
  - The Seraph stops moving for 8 seconds, taking the appearance of being seated. Explosion particles appear centered on the Seraph when this starts.
  - After 2 seconds, it becomes invulnerable and casts 12 Broken Heart Radiation beams over the next 6 seconds: 4 groups of 3 20-block long beams, stacked 1 block on top of each other, each group at 90° to each other. The beams pass through walls. The beams spawn pointing in the cardinal directions, and rotate clockwise for the duration, completing slightly less than three quarters of a full rotation in the 6 seconds. They begin blue and change to purple, then green over the duration. In Minecraft 1.16 and beyond, bubble particles are added.
  - Any player fighting the Seraph who comes into contact with a beam takes 800 [[True Damage]] and has -12 [[Vitality]] for 90s. The [[Vitality]] reduction stacks. In addition, any player fighting the Seraph who steps out of reach of the beams begins to take 10% of their max health as [[True Damage]] twice per second.
  - Yang Glyphs and Nukekubi Fixations can be thrown in this phase. However, Dissonance for the Voidgloom Seraph deals 0 damage during Broken Heart Radiation.
## Minibosses
Slayer Minibosses have a 20% chance to spawn upon gaining [[Combat|Combat XP]] from killing a mob of the corresponding type while having a Tier III or higher [[Slayer|Slayer Quest]] active, assuming the following conditions are met:
- The player has gained at least 19% progress towards the slayer quest since the last miniboss spawn.
- At least 1 second has passed since the last miniboss spawn.
Tier IV and above slayer bosses can spawn two possible types of minibosses: a stronger one and a weaker one. There is a 20% (1 in 5) chance for the miniboss to be the stronger variant.

## Trivia
- The Voidgloom Seraph Tier IV used to have 300M [[Health]], one of the highest [[Health]] of all non-dungeon enemies in the game.
- Nukekubi Fixation heads can be thrown through walls, though Players can still destroy the heads by looking at them.
- Thrown Yang Glyphs can sometimes replace blocks such as stairs, [[End Stone]] and glass panes. When the Glyph is destroyed, the block will not be replaced until the server restarts.
- When using Broken Heart Radiation, the particles from the Voidgloom Seraph teleporting to the player will sometimes show. The Seraph will not move.
- The Voidgloom Seraph's invulnerability during Broken Heart Radiation does not start until the lasers are fired, leaving a brief period of vulnerability in the transition phase.
- If a Voidgloom Seraph Tier IV despawns while attacking with Broken Heart Radiation, the beams will persist indefinitely. These beams are visual only.
- The Voidgloom Seraph's name may be a reference to Seraph of the End.
- The name of Nukekubi Fixations are a reference to nukekubi, a mythical creature in Japanese folklore whose head detaches from its body.
- When in the Malvolent Hitshield, the Voidgloom Seraph can take damage using bows other than the [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]], making it easy to quickly defeat the shield.
- The Voidgloom Seraph does not take damage from water, but attacks less when in water.
- If a player deals enough damage to a Tier IV Voidgloom Seraph just as Broken Heart Radiation triggers to also trigger Malevolent Hitshield, the boss will not throw Yang Glyphs during this phase and immediately start in the Hitshields phase after the lasers complete their full rotation.
- Voidgloom Seraph and the [[Riftstalker Bloodfiend]] are the only two Slayer boss that can be spawned in one type of island, which are [[The End]] and [[Stillgore Château]] respectively.
- It is possible to grief others' if one hits the Voidgloom Seraph only a couple times by using a invincibility item that blocks [[True Damage]] instead of breaking it yourself.

# Volt
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Templates with deprecated argument names, Rift Dimension
[Volt]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 4
Spawn Location: [[Dreadfarm]]
Mob Type: None
Stats: [[Health]]: 155; [[Rift Damage]]: 60
[Drops]
Drops: 1x [[Volt Crux]]
XP: +6 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[Metadata]
ID: VOLT_4
The Volt is a [[Mob]] that spawns in [[Dreadfarm]]. It appears as a floating head.
## Drops
[[Volt Crux]] drops are affected by the [[Crux Talisman]] and its upgrades.
Item drops - [Lv4]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Volt Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv4]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Motes]]|160|160|160|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|6|6|6|
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.
Item drops - [Lv4]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Volt Crux]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
## Behavior
Volts are passive until a player damages them. When hostile, they move towards the player and take 5 [[Rift Time]] from the player on contact. After taking 3 hits from the player, they will become invulnerable and stop moving. While in this state, they will spawn a Block of Emerald nearby with SAFE above it, while charging up their Strikeburst ability. After 2 seconds, lightning will strike the area around them, except for the safe Block of Emerald, giving nearby players a brief period of blindness. The strike can also be avoided by moving far enough away from the Volt. If the player is hit by lightning, they will lose 60s [[Rift Time]]. After the ability is used 3 times, Volts will become vulnerable again.

|May 4, 2023|Added Volt.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

## Trivia
- The Voracious Spider was most likely added as an easier way to complete [[Spider Slayer]] bosses as [[Spider|Spiders]] didn't spawn enough to make the Slayer mission progress at a steady rate.

# Wai
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Huntable Creatures, Crimson Isle
[Wai]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 6
Spawn Location: [[Mystic Marsh]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 100; [[Damage]]: 100
[Drops]
XP: +9 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 6
[Metadata]
ID: ATOM_50
The Wai is a passive [[Mob]].
## Spawning
A Wai spawns when 2 [[Exe|Exes]] come into contact.
## Behavior
Wais float in place at their spawn locations. They cannot be hit. Using a [[Charminizer]] on a Wai will cause it to start following the player around. If 2 Wais come into contact, they will merge into a [[Zee]]. Using an [[Atominizer]] on a Wai will cause it to drop an [[Y]].
## Drops
Non-item drops - [Lv6]:
|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|2|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|9|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|6|
Using an [[Atominizer]] on a Wai will cause it to drop an [[Y]].
Wai can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or caught with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to obtain [[XYZ Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
## Gallery
- The Wai center head

|Aug 25, 2026|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv50] to [Lv6], [[Coins]] from 10 to 2, [[Experience]] from 10 to 6, and [[Combat]] XP from 0 to 9.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]

|Oct 6, 2026|0.27.2|Changed the Mob Level ([[Mob]]) from [Lv50] to [Lv6], [[Coins]] from 10 to 2, [[Experience]] from 10 to 6, and [[Combat]] XP from 0 to 9.|

# Wandering Blaze
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Kuudra
[Wandering Blaze]
Type: [[Mob]]

[Drops]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 189-236
[Metadata]
ID: WANDERING_BLAZE_100; WANDERING_BLAZE_200; WANDERING_BLAZE_300; WANDERING_BLAZE_400; WANDERING_BLAZE_500
The Wandering Blaze is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Wandering Blazes spawn in batches during some wave intervals of five in the [[Kuudra]] fight.
## Behavior
Wandering Blazes will shoot fireballs at players. The damage dealt from the fireballs is based on the tier of [[Kuudra]], as well as the amount of times the player has been hit, which has a cache expiration time of 20 seconds. Damage Dealt=BaseDamage×Hits×0.01
## Variants

|[Lv194]|Burning Tier [[Kuudra]]|7,000,000|550|291|213|64|

ID: WATER_WORM_20
The Water Worm is a [[Rarity|RARE]] [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature]] that can be fished up in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
The Water Worm can be fished up in the [[Goblin Holdout]]. It requires [[Fishing|Fishing XV (15)]] or higher to be fished up.
The base [[Sea Creatures|Weight]] for the Water Worm is 300, resulting in a default catch chance of 6.13% (1 in 16.32 sea creatures) with all Sea Creatures unlocked. Certain modifiers can be used to increase it:
|Condition|Weight|
|-|-|
|[[Whale Bait]], [[Light Bait]], and [[Dark Bait]]|1.25x|
[[Worm Bait]] can be used to only catch Worms.

The Weakling is a type of [[Mob]] and [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblin]] that spawns in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Weaklings spawn during [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raids]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and randomly throughout the [[Goblin Holdout]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Behavior
Weaklings move around slowly compared to other [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblins]], and rarely attack the player.

# Weaver Spider
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Weaver Spider]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 4-9
Spawn Location: [[Spider's Den]]
Mob Type: [[Arthropod]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 160-370; [[Damage]]: 35-65
[Drops]
Drops: 0–1x [[Arachne's Calling]] 0–1x [[Travel Scroll to Spider's Den Top of Nest]] 0–1x [[String]] 0–1x LEGENDARY Tarantula Pet ([[Tarantula Pet]]) 0–1x [[Spider Eye]] 0–1x EPIC Tarantula Pet ([[Tarantula Pet]])
XP: 6-50 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 4-9
[Metadata]
ID: WEAVER_SPIDER_#
The Weaver Spider is a [[Spider]] that can shoot webs that slow the player down for a short amount of time. Weaver Spiders can be found in the [[Spider's Den]], usually higher up in the [[Spider Mound]], and in the [[Arachne's Burrow]].
## Behavior
The Weaver Spider has multiple attacks:
- The Weaver Spider will jump toward the player, inflicting damage.
- The Weaver Spider will shoot a web at the player from a distance, which reduces [[Speed]] by 10 if it hits. [[Speed]] reduction stacks.
Weaver Spiders will shoot webs randomly, even when non-aggressive. These webs will hurt the player if they touch them.

The Wither Sentry is a [[Mob]] that spawns during the [[Kuudra]] fight in [[Kuudra's Hollow]].
## Spawning
Wither Sentries occasionally spawn in batches during new waves in the [[Kuudra]] fight. They can spawn starting from the seventh wave.
## Behavior
Wither Sentries summon wither skulls around themselves, which fly towards nearby players, dealing [[Damage]] on impact.

[Metadata]
ID: WITHER_SKELETON_70
The Wither Skeleton is a [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Stronghold]] in the [[Crimson Isle]]. It has 300K [[Health]], and deals 2000 [[Damage]]. They take extra damage from the [[Wither Bow]].
## Spawning
Wither Skeletons spawn in the [[Stronghold]] in the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Behavior
Wither Skeletons wander when idle, but sprint to attack players from as far as 32 blocks, if their line of sight is not obstructed. When the player is attacked by a Wither Skeleton, they are inflicted with the Wither effect for 10 seconds.

[Metadata]
ID: WITHER_SPECTRE_70
The Wither Spectre is a hostile [[Mob]]. They spawn in the left side of the [[Stronghold]] on the [[Crimson Isle]].
## Behavior
If a player is nearby, Wither Spectres will quickly run towards them. Players hit by a Wither Spectre are temporarily afflicted with the Wither effect. Although Wither Spectres deal 2000 [[Damage]], they are one of the easiest types of monsters to defeat in the Crimson Isle.

The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Trivia
- Wither Spectres are currently one of the best mobs for [[Voidgloom Seraph]] [[Necromancy]], other good ones being [[Minotaur|Minotaurs]] from Legendary [[Griffin Pet]], [[Minos Champion|Minos Champions]] from Epic Griffin Pet and [[Great White Shark|Great White Sharks]] from [[Fishing Festival]].
- Despite American English being more common on Hypixel, this mob is named using British English ("spectre" instead of "specter"). Interestingly, [[Wither Spectre Shard|its Shard]] was named using American English until the 0.27 update.

The Wolf is a [[Mob]] that spawns on the [[Private Island]] on [[Stranded|☀ Stranded]] [[Profile|Profiles]].
## Spawning

# Wraith
Categories: Mobs, Mobs with Bestiary data, Events, Spooky Festival
[Wraith]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 99
Mob Type: [[Spooky]] [[Shielded]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 15; [[Damage]]: 50
[Drops]

XP: +148 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 108
[Metadata]
ID: WRAITH_50
The Wraith is a mob that spawns during [[Spooky Festival|Spooky Festivals]] at night.
## Spawning
Wraiths can spawn in the [[Graveyard]], [[Wilderness]], [[Spider's Den]] and [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] at night during a [[Spooky Festival]]. There is a 5.1% chance of a Wraith spawning from a [[Trick or Treat Chest]].
## Behavior
Wraiths have 15 [[Health]] and deal 50 [[Damage]]. They fly around the player, so they can be very hard to hit. Every attack against them deals 1 of damage. The [[Ghoul Buster]] deals 2 of damage instead.

## Trivia
- The [[Thunderlord]] and [[Thunderbolt]] were previously bugged, and dealt [[True Damage]] to the mob, ignoring their [[Defense]]. This was patched in a later update.
- The Wraith may be a reference to the Wraith in Terraria with similar sounds and texture.
- The wraith is technically a player-model mob. Therefore, it has a body, albeit an invisible one. Because of this, one can aim a hit below its head and deal damage without actually punching the wraith's head itself.

Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, Crystal Hollows

[[Experience]] Orbs: 102
[Metadata]
ID: YOG_100
Yog is a hostile [[Mob]] that spawns in the [[Magma Fields]] and [[Khazad-dûm]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Behavior
Yog hops after the Player, setting off an explosion effecting the blocks around it as it lands. Once a Yog is killed, a red bar appears over their head. After around four seconds, the bar finishes counting down, and they explode, dealing up to 10000 [[Damage]] to nearby players, or instantly killing players directly on the Yog. Drops are calculated after the death explosion, meaning [[Enchantments]] such as [[Looting]] are applied from the item the player is holding after the explosion.

# Zealot
Aliases: Enderchest Zealots, Zealots, Zelot
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, The End, Ender
[Zealot]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 73
Spawn Location: [[Dragon's Nest]]
Mob Type: [[Ender]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 13,000; [[Damage]]: 1250
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Ender Pearl]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] 0–1x [[Pearlescent Dye]]
XP: +109 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 80
[Metadata]
ID: ZEALOT_ENDERMAN_55
Zealots are a type of [[Enderman]] found exclusively in the [[Dragon's Nest]].
## Spawning
Zealots spawn in predetermined locations in the [[Dragon's Nest]]. A wave of 16 Zealots spawns every 11 seconds, except in the period between the death of an [[Ender Dragon]] and the respawning of the [[Dragon Egg]]. Spawned Zealots have a 2% (1 in 50) chance to spawn holding an Ender Chest. These Zealots drop additional loot upon being killed.
|Location|Spawn Spots (The number of spots in the location where this mob can spawn in.)|Spawn Delay (The time, after which a new mob spawns in every spot.)|Max per spot (The maximum number of mobs that can exist in each spot at a time.)|
|-|-|-|-|
|Dragon's Nest|16|11s|2|
### Dragons
An [[Ender Dragon]] can summon large waves of Zealots when it is weak and the battle has gone on for a long time. These Zealots are hostile towards players.
## Behavior
Naturally spawning Zealots are neutral mobs like [[Enderman]]; they do not attack the player unless they are attacked first, or a player looks at their head when provoked. If the player angers them, they will attack the aggressor and regularly teleport behind them to evade attacks. Zealots spawned from [[Ender Dragon|Ender Dragons]] are always hostile. They have 13,000 [[Health]] and deal 1,250 [[Damage]].
Zealots, like [[Enderman]], cannot be damaged by [[Arrows]] from most [[Bows]] or harmful [[Potions]]; attempting to shoot one results in the Zealot teleporting away. However, magic projectiles such as those from the [[Frozen Scythe|Frozen]] and [[Glacial Scythe]], [[Jerry-chine Gun]], and [[Explosive Bow]] can still damage them. The bows obtained from [[Enderman Slayer]], being the [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]], can also damage Zealots.
When an [[Ender Dragon]] is defeated, all Zealots in the Nest, including Special Zealots, immediately despawn.
### Special Zealots
When a player kills a Zealot, there is a chance for a [[Special Zealot]] to spawn nearby.
### End Stone Protector
The [[End Stone Protector]] spawns in a lobby when 5,000 Zealots have been killed since the last boss. Every 1,000 kills, a message is displayed in chat and more layers of the Protector emerge.
Contributing up to 100 Zealot kills to the milestone increases the quality of loot from the [[Bosses|Boss]].

# Zealot Bruiser
Aliases: Bruiser
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data, The End
[Zealot Bruiser]
Type: [[Mob]]
Mob Level: 115
Spawn Location: [[Zealot Bruiser Hideout]]
Mob Type: [[Ender]]
Stats: [[Health]]: 65,000; [[Damage]]: 2,500
[Drops]
Drops: 2–4x [[Ender Pearl]] 0–1x [[Enchanted Ender Pearl]] 0–1x [[Pearlescent Dye]]
XP: +172 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 126
[Metadata]
ID: ZEALOT_BRUISER_100
Zealot Bruisers are a stronger type of [[Zealot]] found exclusively in the [[Zealot Bruiser Hideout]]. They have a higher chance of spawning a [[Special Zealot]]. However, they have more [[Health]] and deal more [[Damage]].
## Behavior
Zealot Bruisers are neutral mobs like [[Enderman|Endermen]]; they do not attack the player unless they are attacked first, or a player looks at their head When provoked. If the player angers them, they will attack the aggressor and regularly teleport behind them to evade attacks. They have 65,000 [[Health]] and deal 2,500 [[Damage]].
Zealot Bruisers, like [[Enderman|Endermen]], cannot be damaged by [[Arrows]] from most [[Bows]] or harmful [[Potions]]; attempting to shoot one results in the Zealot Bruiser teleporting away. However, magic projectiles such as those from the [[Frozen Scythe]], [[Jerry-chine Gun]], and [[Explosive Bow]] can still damage them. Bows from [[Enderman Slayer]] such as the [[Juju Shortbow]] and [[Terminator]] are also able to damage them.
### Special Zealots
When a player kills a Zealot Bruiser, there is a chance for a [[Special Zealot]] to spawn nearby.
## Drops

|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|

|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|126|126|126|
Zealot Bruiser can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Zealot Bruiser Shard]] and +150 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+1,500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].
### Loot Sharing
Mob drops are rolled from this loot pool if the player [[Loot Sharing|Loot Shares]] drops from the Mob.

# Zee
Categories: Mobs, Mobs without Bestiary data, Huntable Creatures, Crimson Isle
[Zee]
Type: [[Mob]]

Spawn Location: [[Mystic Marsh]]
Mob Type: [[Arcane]]

[Drops]
XP: +9 Combat XP ([[Combat]])
[[Experience]] Orbs: 6
[Metadata]
ID: ATOM_50
The Zee is a passive [[Mob]].
## Spawning
A Zee spawns when 2 [[Wai|Wais]] come into contact.
## Behavior
Zees float in place at their spawn locations. They cannot be hit. Using a [[Charminizer]] on a Zee will cause it to start following the player around. Using an [[Atominizer]] on a Zee will cause it to drop a [[Z]].
## Drops

|Drop|Count|
|-|-|
|[[Coins]]|2|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|9|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|6|
Using an [[Atominizer]] on a Zee will cause it to drop a [[Z]].
Zee can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or caught with a [[Lassos|Lasso]] to obtain [[XYZ Shard]] and +500 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+5,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

# Zombuddy
Categories: Mobs, Huntable Creatures, Mobs with Bestiary data
[Zombuddy]

[[Experience]] Orbs: 71
[Metadata]
ID: ZOMBUDDY_1
A Zombuddy is a [[Mob]] that spawns from harvesting [[Zombud]] in [[The Garden]].
## Spawning
When harvesting [[Zombud]], the mutation spawns a Zombuddy in place of every adjacent [[Dead Plant]]. Up to 8 Zombuddies can be spawned from a single harvested [[Zombud]].
## Behavior
A Zombuddy walks towards the player to attack.
## Drops
Item drops - [Lv65]:
|Drop|Count|Drop Rarity|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Zombud]]|1|2Common Loot ([[Drop Chance]])|100%|
[Non-item drops - [Lv65]]
|Drop|Count|Count|Count|
|[[Coins]]|21|21|21|
|Combat XP ([[Combat]])|97|97|97|
|Experience Orbs ([[Experience]])|71|71|71|
Zombuddy can also be [[Charm Chance|Charmed]] or put in a [[Black Holes|Black Hole]] to obtain [[Zombuddy Shard]] and +300 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] (+3,000 [[Hunting|Hunting XP]] when [[Charm Chance|Charmed]]) per shard. The amount of [[Attributes|Attribute Shards]] dropped is affected by [[Hunting Fortune]].

## Trivia
- Despite being in the [[The Garden]] [[Bestiary]] category, Zombuddies are immune to [[Vacuums]] and don't grant a [[Pests (Currency)|Pest]] when slain.
  - However, each [[Bestiary]] tier still grants 0.4 [[Farming Fortune]].
- The Zombuddy's lore in the [[Bestiary]], "Zombuddy that I used to know", is a play on Somebody That I Used to Know, a 2011 song by singer-songwriter Gotye.

The Creeperlobber is a type of [[Mob]] and [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblin]] that spawns in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Creeperlobbers spawn during [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raids]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]], as well as in the [[Goblin Holdout]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] in certain structures.
## Variants
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv25]|Goblin Raids ([[Goblin Raid]]), [[Goblin Holdout]]|30,000|10|37|27|8|GOBLIN_CREEPERTAMER_90|
|[Lv89]|[[Goblin Holdout]]|60,000|700|133|97|29|GOBLIN_CREEPERTAMER_100|
## Behavior
Creeperlobbers throw a [[Creeper (Creeperlobber)|Creeper]] towards nearby players every 4.5 seconds. These creepers explode shortly after, dealing 50 [[Damage]] plus 20% of the player's maximum [[Health]] as [[True Damage]]. They only have 2 [[Health]], meaning they die in 2 hits, and they despawn automatically after 25 seconds or when the Creeperlobber dies.

The Fireslinger is a type of [[Mob]] and [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblin]] that spawns in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Fireslingers spawn naturally in the [[Goblin Holdout]] in the [[Crystal Hollows]], in the [[Goblin Burrows]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]], and during [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raids]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].

|[Lv23]|[[Goblin Burrows]]|800|300|34|25|7|GOBLIN_WEAKLING_BOW_25|
## Behavior
Fireslingers throw fireballs that sets the player on fire, whilst also running away from players.

Hidden Jerries are mobs that have a chance of appearing when a [[Player]] earns [[Skills|Skill XP]] through killing mobs, mining, farming, or foraging while [[Candidate Jerry|Jerry]] is elected as mayor.
## Spawning
Hidden Jerry can spawn while [[Candidate Jerry]] is in office and player gains Skill XP. Chance to spawn depends on the activity:
|Condition|Chance|
|-|-|
|Crop broken|0.04% (1 in 2,500 crops)|
|Mining block broken|0.33% (1 in 300 blocks)|
|Foraging block broken|0.166% (1 in 600 blocks)|
|Mob killed|1% (1 in 100 mobs)|
Hidden Jerry spawns have a 6 minute cooldown. Player profile must have at least 4 hours of playtime to spawn Hidden Jerries.
There are 4 types of Hidden Jerries:
|Level|Name|Spawn Chance|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[Lv25]|Green Jerry|54.55% (1 in 1.83) (12 weight)|3|1|37|27|8|MAYOR_JERRY_GREEN_1|
|[Lv27]|Blue Jerry|27.27% (1 in 3.67) (6 weight)|4|1|40|29|9|MAYOR_JERRY_BLUE_2|
|[Lv29]|Purple Jerry|13.64% (1 in 7.33) (3 weight)|5|1|43|31|9|MAYOR_JERRY_PURPLE_3|
|[Lv31]|Golden Jerry|4.55% (1 in 22) (1 weight)|6|1|46|34|10|MAYOR_JERRY_GOLDEN_5|
Golden Jerry spawn chance can be increased with [[Tracking]].
## Behavior
When a Hidden Jerry is found a message will appear in your chat letting you know that a Jerry has spawned. There will be a floating head that will fly around for about 3 seconds before spawning a Hidden Jerry. Once spawned, the Jerry will quickly run away, sometimes falling into the void or off a high area. Using a bow from a distance can however be used to shoot the Jerry without it running away.
Being [[Shielded]], Hidden Jerries can only take 1 [[Damage]] per hit. They can be damaged by melee attacks and [[Bows]], but not [[Mage]] weapons. All have the possibility to be runic. When killed, they drop a [[Jerry Box]] that corresponds to the color (for example, Golden Jerry drops the Golden [[Jerry Box]]). The summoner has a drop priority for 25 seconds[Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)], after which anyone who kills Hidden Jerry will obtain the box.

## Tips
- An efficient way to spawn Hidden Jerries is mining [[Cobblestone]], [[Sand]], [[Mycelium]], or [[Red Sand]] with [[Mineral Armor]] or [[Glossy Mineral Armor]].
  - Foraging with high amounts of [[Sweep]] and Farming are also viable, but the former method results in more effective BPS than normally possible in vanilla Minecraft, and therefore are better at spawning Hidden Jerries.
## Trivia
- When Hidden Jerries spawn, the box that initially appears displays a name tag of "Mayor Jerry's".
- If the player's Hidden Jerry dies due to fall damage, they still drop a [[Jerry Box]] as long as the player had hit it at least once.
- Like [[Spooky]] [[Mob|Mobs]], the [[Thunderlord]] and [[Thunderbolt]] enchantments do not bypass their defense.
- Like most other [[Mob|Mobs]], Hidden Jerries can be [[Runecrafting|Runic]], meaning they will take more hits to kill.
  - The nametag of [[Runecrafting|Runic]] Hidden Jerries are not purple like other [[Runes|Runic Mobs]].
- [[Fishing Rods]] could at some point bypass their [[Defense]].
- If a Hidden Jerry is spawned inside [[Dungeons]], it is instantly killed if the player hits it.
- Hidden Jerries can open doors due to their villager entity. This then gets reverted server-side, leading to potential ghost doors.
  - This can lead to decreased efficiency on the [[The Garden]] if doors are used in farm designs.
- If a Jerry is [[Cocooning|Cocooned]], it will no longer show the name of the player that spawned it.

The Murderlover is a type of [[Mob]] and [[Goblin (disambiguation)|Goblin]] that spawns in the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Spawning
Murderlovers spawn during [[Goblin Raid|Goblin Raids]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]], as well as in the [[Goblin Holdout]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] in certain structures.
## Variants
|Level|Spawn Location|Stats [[Health]]|Stats [[Damage]]|Drops Combat XP ([[Combat]])|Drops [[Experience]]|Drops [[Coins]]|Internal IDs|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|[Lv96]|[[Goblin Holdout]]|80,000|800|144|105|32|GOBLIN_MURDERLOVER_200|
## Behavior
Murderlovers attack the player from a distance using a fishing rod, dealing high amounts of [[Damage]] on impact. They also strafe sideways to avoid being hit as easily. They cannot be attacked from players with less than 1,500 [[Effective Health]].

# Blocks
Aliases: Block
Categories: More info needed, Blocks
Blocks are mineable blocks that spawn in certain [[Locations]]. They are affected by [[Block Fortune]] and can be found in many different places, usually inaccessible before reaching certain Skill levels.
## Obtaining
Blocks can be found in many different locations ([[Hub Island|Hub]], [[Gold Mine]], [[Deep Caverns]], [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Mushroom Desert]], [[Crimson Isle]]).
Unlike most [[Ores]], [[Dwarven Metals]], and [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], most Blocks do not require [[Breaking Power]] to be mined.

# Breakable Blocks
Aliases: Farming Blocks, Foraging Blocks, Mining Blocks
Categories: More info needed
Breakable Blocks in SkyBlock refer to physical blocks in the world that can be broken, which are mostly associated with the [[Mining]], [[Foraging]], and [[Farming]] skills. They can also refer to certain items that come from breaking these blocks.
## Mining Blocks
There are five official classifications of Breakable Blocks related to the [[Mining]] skill, four of which are affected by specific [[Stats]] when broken:
|Name|Affecting Stats|
|-|-|
|Ores|[[Ore Fortune]]; [[Mining Fortune]]; [[Mining Spread]]|
|Blocks|[[Block Fortune]]; [[Mining Fortune]]; [[Mining Spread]]|
|Dwarven Metals|[[Dwarven Metal Fortune]]; [[Mining Fortune]]; [[Mining Spread]]|
|Gemstones|[[Gemstone Fortune]]; [[Mining Fortune]]; [[Gemstone Spread]]; [[Pristine]]|
|Miscellaneous||
### Ores
These Breakable Blocks are affected by [[Ore Fortune]], [[Mining Fortune]], and [[Mining Spread]].

### Blocks
These Breakable Blocks are affected by [[Block Fortune]], [[Mining Fortune]], and [[Mining Spread]].
Blocks that do not have a minimum [[Breaking Power]] can be broken without holding a tool, and its resources will still be granted.
Similarly, blocks that don't have a set amount of [[Mining Speed]] to be instantly broken are like this because they're not buffed when holding a [[Mining]] tool ([[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]], [[Drills|Drill]], or [[Gauntlet]]). These types of blocks use the Vanilla Dig Speed Calculation, which does not consider [[Mining Speed]] when broken.

# Catacombs Puzzle Rooms
Aliases: Creeper Beams, Dungeon Puzzle, Higher or Lower, Oruo, Oruo the Omniscient, Puzzle Room, Puzzle room, Quiz, The Catacombs Puzzle Rooms
Categories: More info needed, Mechanics, Dungeons, Catacombs, Locations
On each floor, 2 or more puzzles will generate. They are marked as purple rooms on the [[Magical Map]]. Two of the same puzzle room will never generate in the same dungeon.
Each puzzle room contains a simple puzzle for players to solve. Completing a puzzle rewards the team with a Tier V [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]], as well as marking the puzzle on the Magical Map as complete. Failing to complete a puzzle subtracts 14 points from the team's Skill score unless a player uses the [[Architect's First Draft]] to reset the puzzle.
The status of puzzles in the current Dungeon can be found in the tab menu. There, players can see the total number of puzzles in the Dungeon, their names, their states (incomplete, complete, or failed), and if failed, who was responsible.
Many popular SkyBlock mods have solvers that could make doing puzzles significantly easier, such as highlighting the correct Blaze to hit, showing the correct pathway to take for the Ice puzzles etc.
## List of Puzzles Rooms
### Tic Tac Toe
[Tic Tac Toe]
Dungeon Floor: Any
Failable: ✔ Yes
To solve, players must tie a Tic Tac Toe match against an AI. When the player enters the room, the AI always starts first. In floors 0-3 it always starts in the middle, while in later floors it starts either in the middle or in one of the corners.
If the AI starts in the middle, pick any corner first. If the AI starts in the corner, choose the middle first. Then, block any 3-in-a-row the AI tries to make.
In this room there is also a secret chest, located inside a wall to the left of the main puzzle. To access that chest, go up the stairs in the back of the room and turn the lever. The wall will open, and the chest will be accessible. The open wall has leaves near it.
### Three Weirdos
[Three Weirdos]
Dungeon Floor: Any
Failable: ✔ Yes
Three NPCs stand in a room with three corresponding chests. They will make statements about who has the correct chest, but one or more are lying. Players must use the given information to open the correct chest.
Show/Hide Solutions
Case 1:
- [Person 1]: The reward is not in my chest!
- [Person 2]: One of us is telling the truth!
- [Person 3]: They are both telling the truth. The reward isn't in [Person 1]'s chest.
Case 2:
- [Person 1]: We are all telling the truth!
- [Person 2]: At least one of them is lying, and the reward is not in [Person 3]'s chest.
- [Person 3]: [Person 1] is telling the truth and the reward is in his chest.
Case 3:
- [Person 1]: My chest doesn't have the reward. At least one of the others is telling the truth!
- [Person 2]: My chest doesn't have the reward we are all telling the truth.
- [Person 3]: One of the others is lying.
Case 4:
- [Person 1]: My chest has the reward and I'm telling the truth!
- [Person 2]: They are both telling the truth, the reward is in [Person 3]'s chest.
- [Person 3]: They are both lying, the reward is in my chest!
Case 5:
- [Person 1]: The reward isn't in any of our chests.
- [Person 2]: The reward is in my chest.
- [Person 3]: The reward is not in my chest. They are both lying.
Case 6:
- [Person 1]: [Person 2] is telling the truth.
- [Person 2]: My chest has the reward.
- [Person 3]: Both of them are telling the truth. Also, [Person 1] has the reward in their chest.
### Creeper Beams
[Creeper Beams]
Dungeon Floor: Any
Failable: ✕ No
Sea Lanterns are placed on the walls and ceiling of the room. They can be joined with a beam by shooting them with a bow. Players must get four different beams to pass through the Creeper in the middle to complete the room. To do so, Players have to look for two lanterns that are placed at two other sides of the room. When all four beams are aligned properly, the Creeper explodes, revealing a treasure chest containing a level V [[Dungeon Blessing|Blessing]].
### Water Board
[Water Board]
Dungeon Floor: Any
Failable: ✕ No
Three of the five doors to the chest are closed. By making the water flow into the hole with the corresponding color on the water maze, players can toggle the doors open and closed. Six levers retract and extend their corresponding blocks on the water maze, and another lever located at the back of the room toggles the flow of water. Players must use the levers to control the flow of water to enter the correct holes and have all five doors open at the same time.
If the water flows into a hole that corresponds to an already opened door, the door will close. This may lead to the Player being locked inside. There are a few ways to escape, mainly:
- Another player opening the closed door.
- Using a [[Spirit Leap]].
- Using the Ether Transmission Ability provided by the [[Etherwarp Conduit]].
- Leaving the dungeon and having the party leader warp the Player back. This is however recommended only if other options are unavailable, as it will count as a death. This means that after coming back, the Player will end up as a ghost.
### Teleport Maze
[Teleport Maze]
Dungeon Floor: 1 and Above
Failable: ✕ No
In the teleport maze your goal is to get to the center room by using teleport pads. After using a teleport pad, you face towards the teleporter that will take you to the final room. However, this pad is often in a different room.
The best way to finish quickly, is by running to the teleport pad diagonal of the one you used to enter the room. Keep running diagonal until you spawn facing a teleport pad in the same room. This teleporter should take you to the center.
### Boulder
In the Boulder Puzzle, you arrive in a room, and after taking the stairs down, it is filled with "boxes"(3x3x3 blocks of wood planks with buttons) in different combinations. Trying to go over the boxes makes white stained glass appear under you, so you cannot just go over the boxes.
To complete this puzzle, you have to press the buttons to make the box go to the side opposite the button. If the box is at the very back and you press the button, the box will disappear. If you push a box into another box, you cannot move it the other way. Normally, you are able to complete the puzzle by pushing only a couple boxes, usually at the sides, so it isn't 'hard', but it takes experience to do it without much thought.
### Higher or Lower
[Higher or Lower]
Dungeon Floor: 3 and Above
Failable: ✔ Yes
With Higher or Lower, also known as the Blaze Puzzle, you arrive in a tall chamber with blazes and a ring spiraling on the edges that you can use to climb up.
To successfully complete this puzzle, you have to kill all the blazes in a specific order.
- If the chest spawns at the bottom of the chamber, then you have to kill the [[Blaze (Higher or Lower)|Blazes]] starting with the lowest health and slowly advancing to the highest health.
- If the chest spawns at the top of the chamber, then you have to kill the [[Blaze (Higher or Lower)|Blazes]] starting with the highest health and slowly advancing to the lowest health.
- If you kill a [[Blaze (Higher or Lower)|Blaze]] out of order, you fail the puzzle.
Note : caution should be taken by avoiding the following :
- Bows like [[Runaan's Bow]] or the [[Hurricane Bow]] due to them shooting multiple arrows at once.
- Both [[Frozen Scythe]] and [[Glacial Scythe]], due to their innate piercing ability.
- Enchants like [[Duplex]] should be not be used.[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
- Classes : [[Archer|Archers]] should avoid this puzzle with their bows due to their passive ability, Bouncy Arrows, which can easily cause the puzzle to fail. However, they can use fishing weapons to throw hooks and kill the blazes.
### Ice Fill
In this puzzle, there are three levels of ice. You must walk through each ice block to turn them into packed ice blocks. Stepping on the block you already stepped on earlier, jumping, or moving diagonally causes the ice to shatter, forcing you to repeat the puzzle. This puzzle only spawns on Floor 7 and rewards two [[Dungeon Blessing|blessings]].
### Ice Path
In this puzzle, there's a silverfish with TNT on its head. You must guide it to the chest by punching it in a direction. Once punched, it won't stop sliding until it hits a wall or a block. Do note that trying to cheese this puzzle by placing Traps would cause the puzzle to be failed.
### Quiz
In this puzzle, a statue called Oruo the Omniscient will ask three SkyBlock-related questions, such as Catacombs Boss titles, Fairy Souls, etc. If one clicks a potential answer in the chat, it'll tell that you are suggesting that it's the answer.
Once completed, the puzzle grants a unique Blessing of Time granting [[Health]], [[Defense]], [[Intelligence]] and [[Strength]]. On top of that, Oruo will also add 1 hours to player's [[Bottle of Jyrre]] and [[Dark Cacao Truffle]] and max out their [[Training Weights]].

### Removed Rooms
#### Bomb Defuse
Bomb Defuse was a dungeon puzzle involving a creeper at the center with two chests. At both sides of the room lies two pressure plates that two players must step on. Once stepped, a timer counts down where the player on the left has the information to solve the second player's puzzle. If they don't complete the puzzle on time, the creeper explodes, failing the room.
Bomb Defuse was removed on the 0.20.5 update.
## Trivia
- There are a couple of known ways to glitch through blocks and walls:
  - Break a 2x1x1 section of blocks. They will be replaced, but you're sometimes able to glitch in them due to lag. If you keep running into them and breaking more blocks, those other blocks will also disappear. This can be used to cheese Trap Room and Boulder.
  - Using the [[Wither Cloak Sword]] and [[Shadow Fury]], you can teleport to the creepers from the Wither Cloak Sword which are behind a wall, effectively teleporting you behind the wall.
- Oruo is referenced by [[Julian]] in [[Stillgore Château]].
- One of the lanterns in the Creeper Beams room is currently glitched; Appearing to have arrows stuck in it, but doesn't change no matter how many times it is struck. This can be bypassed with [[Arrows|Explosive Arrows]].

# Centauri
Categories: More info needed, AHN Exclusive, Non-player characters, Pages with UIs, Temp/Using Skill Aliases
This page contains content that is exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]] and will never be released on the main Hypixel network.
[Centauri]
Type: [[NPC]]
Location: [[Village]]
Coordinates: 2 77 -5
Centauri is an [[NPC]] exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Network]]. They are located in front of the [[Community Center]], and grant [[Coins]], [[Booster Cookie|Booster Cookies]], item upgrades and update-featured items.
## Interface
Tip: click on certain slots to change the UI or go to another article!
Each new Alpha introduces it's own set of featured items related to the update in testing. Each Alpha also introduces new conveniences to the toy box to make testing easier.

## Usage
Centauri can be accessed by talking to them in the Hub, or via the /centauri command anywhere.
The interface has three parts.
### Featured Update Items
Claim featured items related to this update.
This menu is a shop that sells items featured in an update for 1 [[Coins|Coin]] each.
### Coin Generator
This menu gives player any amount of money. Available options are: 1,000 [[Coins]], 10,000 [[Coins]], 100,000 [[Coins]], 1,000,000 [[Coins]], 10,000,000 [[Coins]], 100,000,000 [[Coins]], 1,000,000,000 [[Coins]], and "Custom Amount".
### Toy Box
A variety of useful and cheaty utilities to make your life easier!
This menu contains other features available to the player. These features applies an effect to a player, and are claimable for any amount of times and without cool down.
|Feature|Description|
|-|-|
|Cookie Jar|When clicked, it gives the player the effect of a [[Booster Cookie]], as if the player has consumed one.; Duration granted: 292,471,155 years, 208 days, 9 hours and 15 minutes in real-life time|
|Potion Seller|When clicked, it gives the player all potion effects of a [[God Potion]], as if the player has consumed one.; Duration granted: 292,471,155 years, 208 days, 9 hours and 15 minutes in real-life time|
|Max Items|When clicked, it makes the following changes to all armor pieces and items in the player's inventory (not including items in [[Storage]] and [[Wardrobe]]) if applicable: Maximum [[Enchantments]] Recombobulate ([[Recombobulator 3000]]) A fixed Reforge ([[Reforging]]) depending on the item's type, overriding any existing reforge on the item. If there is no set reforge for that category, the previous reforge, if any, stays on the item. Currently, this feature does not change any [[Gemstone]] slot on items.|
|Duplicate Items|When clicked when holding an item, it duplicates the item in the player's inventory.|
|Max Collections|When clicked, for all Collection ([[Collections]]) items that has not reached its maximum tier, the collection amount is set to the requirement for the highest tier, meaning all tiers are unlocked.|
|Max Skills|When clicked, for all [[Skills]] that has not reached its maximum level except [[Dungeoneering]], its skill XP is set to an arbitrarily high number, meaning all levels are unlocked.|
|Max Slayers|When clicked, all Slayers ([[Slayer]]) are increased to their maximum level.|
|50k HOTM EXP|When clicked, the player instantly gains 50,000 HotM EXP ([[Heart of the Mountain]]).|
|Big 'ol chunk o' Powder!|When clicked, the player instantly gains 2,000,000 Mithril ([[Mithril Powder]]) and Gemstone ([[Gemstone Powder]]) powder.|
|5k Gems|When clicked, the player's Gems ([[SkyBlock Gems]]) purse is set to 5,000.|
|Set RNG Meter Progress|When clicked, the player is prompted to enter an amount of XP for all the player's RNG Meters ([[Pity and RNG Meters]]) progress to be set to.|
|Clear RNG Meter Progress|When clicked, the player's RNG Meters' progress is set to 0.|
|Fill Agatha's Rewards|Gives you rewards for [[Agatha]]'s contests as if you had participated in 25 of them.|
|Big 'ol chunk o' Whispers!|When clicked, the player instantly gains 1,000,000 Forest Whispers.|
|Shards!|When clicked, the player instantly gains 10 of every available Attribute Shard ([[Attributes]]).|
#### Reforges
Centauri applies the following reforges when using Max Items:
|Type|Reforge|
|-|-|
|[[Armor]]|Ancient ([[Precursor Gear]])|
|Axe ([[Axes]])||
|Bow ([[Bows]])|Precise ([[Optical Lens]])|
|[[Equipment]]||

|Pickaxe ([[Pickaxes]])/Drill ([[Drills]])||
|Sword ([[Swords]])/Fishing Weapon ([[Fishing Rods]])|Fabled ([[Dragon Claw]])|
|Wand ([[Wands]])||

## Trivia
- Centauri's comments when the player claims potion effects is a reference to the infamous potion seller meme.
- Centauri's skin is based off Xûr from the Destiny franchise.
- Centauri and the fact that they are exclusive to the [[Alpha Hypixel Network|Alpha Network]] is a reference to the triple star system Alpha Centauri.
- The line "I solemnly swear I am up to no good.", despite not being verbatim, comes from Harry Potter, where it was used to access the Marauder's Map.
- The max collection feature works for [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] and can be clicked multiple times to max the collections over and over.
- The maximum number of coins that can be generated at once is 263−1=9,223,372,036,854,775,807 (9.223 quintillion or 9.223372e+18), which is the 64-bit signed integer limit. Attempting to generate more coins than this will cap it to this value. However, this can be repeated multiple times, and the player's purse can hold more than that.
- It is possible to generate a negative amount of coins, which will take coins from your purse instead of adding to it.
  - It used to be possible to make your purse go negative with this, but that appears to have been patched.
- You can duplicate an item even if your inventory is full. It will appear to succeed, but it will actually silently fail rather than being added to your [[Stash|Item Stash]] or [[Stash|Material Stash]].

# Coins/Losing
Categories: More info needed
Upon death the player will lose half of the [[Coins]] in their [[Purse]], splitting [[Coins]] into decimals rather than rounding. If the player dies in certain ways, their purse will not be deducted. [[Coins]] are not removed from the purse if the player:
- Falls in the void on a [[Private Island]] or [[The Garden]], without being hit by a mob. (Note that other deaths, including Fall Damage, will still deduct [[Coins]])
- Dies during the [[Trials of Fire]] (with one exception, see [[Trials of Fire|Trials of Fire#Bugs]])
- Dies from drowning on the islands of [[Galatea]]
- Dies in [[Dungeons]], [[Kuudra]], the [[Rift Dimension]], in [[Glacite Mineshafts]] (both to [[Cold]] and combat. Notably, dying in [[Glacite Tunnels]], regardless of the cause, will remove [[Coins]].) and the [[Critter Safari|Safari Zone]]
[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; Matriarch, Aranya; When this info is added, remove this.)
Dying in the previously mentioned also won't damage [[Piggy Bank]].
Reducing [[Coins]] loss on Death:
|Icon|Method|Reduction|Notes|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Phoenix Pet]]|VERY SPECIAL ([[Rarity]]) [[Phoenix Pet]]|100%||
|[[Booster Cookie]]|[[Booster Cookie]]|100%||
||Wolf Slayer Lvl VIII (8) ([[Wolf Slayer]])|5%|Stacks additively with other forms of [[Coins]] loss reduction.|
|[[Piggy Bank]]|[[Piggy Bank]]|100%|Only triggers when losing over 20,000 [[Coins]] (40,000 [[Coins]] or more in purse). Turns into a Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) on death.|
|[[Piggy Bank]]|Cracked Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]])|75%|Only triggers when losing over 20,000 [[Coins]] (40,000 [[Coins]] or more in purse). Turns into a Broken Piggy Bank ([[Piggy Bank]]) on death.|
|[[Bank (Enchantment)]]|Bank ([[Bank (Enchantment)]])|10-50%|Having 2 [[Armor]] pieces with Bank V ([[Bank (Enchantment)]]) will fully negate loss of [[Coins]]. Same applies for any other combination that results in the total level of the enchantments being 10 or higher.|
Players can also prevent losing their [[Coins]] by storing them in a [[Bank]].
Means that prevent death altogether will also prevent losing coins. This includes [[Spirit Mask]], [[Saving Grace]], [[Remnant of the Eye]] and [[Rarity|SPECIAL]] [[Phoenix Pet]] (as [[Rarity|VERY SPECIAL]] always prevents losing coins).

# Equipment Wardrobe
Categories: More info needed, Pages with UIs
[Equipment Wardrobe]
The Equipment Wardrobe is a storage system for [[Equipment]], similar to the [[Wardrobe]]. It can be accessed via /equipment and /eq.
Equipment Wardrobe is also used as a part of [[Loadouts]] system.
## Overview
Players can place different pieces of [[Equipment]] into the Equipment Wardrobe: they can choose between placing a full set or specific pieces of Equipment in each slot, and cannot modify their currently equipped set.
Amount of Equipment Wardrobe Slots unlocked depends on the player's [[Ranks|Rank]].
|Rank|Slots|
|-|-|
|1Default ([[Ranks]])|2|
|2[VIP] ([[Ranks]])|4|
|3[VIP+] ([[Ranks]])|5|
|4[MVP] ([[Ranks]])|7|
|5[MVP+] ([[Ranks]])|9|
Equipment Wardrobe Slots can also be upgraded from the [[Community Center]]:
|Slot|Price|Duration|
|-|-|-|
|1|250 [[SkyBlock Gems]]|6d|
|2|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|[More Info Needed]|

|4|[More Info Needed]|[More Info Needed]|

## Bugs
• When viewing [[Equipment]] pieces in the Equipment Wardrobe, the tooltip says "Right-click to equip!" even though right-clicking the item does nothing. This is not an issue in the [[Equipment Menu]].

# Events
Aliases: Event, Festival, Mayor Event, Timer, Timers
Categories: More info needed, Pages with UIs, Events, Mechanics, Quests
[Events]
Events take place throughout SkyBlock. They can be accessed by opening the [[SkyBlock Menu]] and clicking [[Calendar|Calendar and Events]].
A [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Year]] is exactly 5 days and 4 hours, meaning the timing of events rotates to suit different timezones.
## Yearly Events
Multiple Events take place at the same in-game time every SkyBlock year. Some events are held in the [[Event Square]], while others are held elsewhere.
|Icon|Event|Duration|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Hoppity]]|[[Hoppity's Hunt]]|Early Spring 1 - Late Spring 31|Help [[Hoppity]] find his [[Chocolate Rabbits]] across SkyBlock.|
|[[Mayor Election]]|Election Over ([[Mayor Election]])|Late Spring 27|The Mayor is selected until the next election ends!|
|[[Traveling Zoo]]|[[Traveling Zoo]]|Early Summer 1-3 Early Winter 1-3|Oringo the Traveling Zookeper ([[Oringo]]) is visiting SkyBlock with pets to trade!|
|[[Mayor Election]]|Election Booth Opens ([[Mayor Election]])|Late Summer 27|The Mayor election booth opens in the community center!|
|[[Harvest Feast]]|[[Harvest Feast]]|Early Autumn 1 - Late Autumn 31|4 crops will be in-season and have a chance to drop [[Seasoning]] and [[Rare Crops]]. Donate [[Seasoning]] to the [[Communal Stew]] and be rewarded with Feast Flasks ([[Feast Flask]])!|
|[[Spooky Festival]]|[[Spooky Festival]]|Autumn 29-31|Autumn is in full swing and the air is full of fright. Mob drops have a chance to contain Candy ([[Candy (disambiguation)]]), which can be traded with the [[Fear Mongerer]] for rare items!|
|[[Season of Jerry]]|[[Season of Jerry]]|Late Winter 24-26|The Jerrys are hard at work trying to craft enough Gifts for all of SkyBlock, but an army of enemies are on the attack! Help protect [[Jerry's Workshop]] so that everyone can go home with Gifts!|
|[[New Year Celebration]]|[[New Year Celebration]]|Late Winter 29-31|To celebrate the SkyBlock New Year, the [[Baker]] is giving out free Cake ([[New Year Cake]]).|
### Yearly Events System
These events appear in a 12 year cycle. The year's shop can be located at the [[Hub Well]].
|Icon|Event|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[Year of the Pig]]|[[Year of the Pig]]|Celebrate the Year of the Pig by chasing Shiny Pigs and collecting their treasures to unlock exclusive rewards!|
|[[Year of the Seal]]|[[Year of the Seal]]|The Year of the Seal event primarily consists of [[Fishing]], playing the Beach Ball Minigame, and spending [[Fishy Treat]] at [[Lukas the Aquarist]].|
|[[Year of the Witch]]|[[Year of the Witch]]|The Year of the Witch event primarily consists of brewing Witches Stews ([[Year of the Witch]]), collecting special ingredients ([[Year of the Witch]]) from mobs, and spending Ingredients ([[Year of the Witch]]) at [[Agnes]] and [[Alcina]].|
### Calendar Events
These events only appear when looking directly into the calendar and are not shown in the Events menu, because they are far more common. Major events however appear in Calendar as well as the Events menu.
|Icon|Event|Schedule|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Jacob's Farming Contest]]|[[Jacob's Farming Contest]]|Every 3 days, lasts from XX:15 to XX:35 in real life's time.|Allows players to compete in 3 randomly chosen crops. Participants are awarded Jacob's Tickets ([[Jacob's Ticket]]) and [[Medals]].|
|[[Starlyn Contest]]|Starlyn Contests ([[Starlyn Contest]])|Every day in-game.|Allows players to complete various tasks on [[Galatea]] islands to achieve certain brackets. Participants are awarded Agatha's Coupons ([[Agatha's Coupon]]) and other rewards.|
|[[Dark Auction]]|[[Dark Auction]]|Every 3 days, starts at XX:55 in real life's time.|Allows players to bid against each other for unique items.|
### Mayor Events
Those events occur only when a [[Mayor Election|Mayor]] or [[Mayor Election|Minister]] is elected with the corresponding perk.
|Icon|Event|Mayor|Time|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Mythological Ritual]]|[[Mythological Ritual]]|[[Diana]]|During the entire mayor's tenure.|Dig out [[Griffin Burrows]] and spawn Mythological Creatures ([[Mythological]]).|
|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Marina]]|1st to 3rd of each SkyBlock Month.|Fish up Sharks ([[Sharks (disambiguation)]]).|
|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Fishing Festival]]|[[Foxy]]|22 of Late Summer to 24 of Late Summer.|Fish up Sharks ([[Sharks (disambiguation)]]).|
|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Cole]]|During the entire mayor's tenure.|Get +25 [[Mining Wisdom]], +25% [[Mining Fortune]], and obtain Refined Minerals ([[Refined Mineral]]) and Glossy Gemstones ([[Glossy Gemstone]]).|
|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Mining Fiesta]]|[[Foxy]]|22 of Late Summer to 28 of Late Summer.|(same as above)|
|[[Carnival]]|[[Carnival]]|[[Foxy]]|During the entire mayor's tenure|Play minigames to earn [[Carnival Tokens]], allowing you to purchase [[Carnival Masks]] and Event Perks.|
|[[Richard]]|Stock Exchange ([[Stock of Stonks]])|[[Diaz]]|From 27 of Late Spring to 31 of Late Spring, then repeating every SkyBlock Month for their entire duration, until the end of the mayor’s tenure|Participate in the Stonks Auction for a chance to win [[Stock of Stonks]]! Trade them for extravagant items at the [[Trade Center]].|
### Annual Events
These events happen using the real-life Gregorian calendar.
|Icon|Event|Date|Description|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Tyashoi Alchemist]]|The Great Spook ([[Tyashoi Alchemist]])|October (Varies)|Complete quests to earn limited time rewards based around the [[Fear]] mechanic.|
|[[SkyBlock Anniversary]]|Party Time! ([[SkyBlock Anniversary]])|June 11th (annually)|Talk with [[Simon]] to receive a Crab Hat ([[Crab Hat of Celebration]]). 2020: The event lasted for 24 hours, starting at 11PM EDT on June 10, 2020. 2021: The event did not happen in 2021. 2022: The event lasted for 48 hours, starting at 6:35PM EDT on June 10, 2022. 2023: The event lasted for 168 hours, starting at 6:15PM EDT on June 12, 2023. 2024: The event lasted for 168 hours, starting at 11:55AM EDT on June 11,2024. 2025: [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) 2026: [More Info Needed]|
|[[Udel]]|Udel's Abiphone Keynote ([[Udel]])|During Apple events|Visit [[Udel]]'s shop to attend the new Abiphone presentation!|

## Interface
The Event Rewards interface is for players to collect event rewards that were not yet collected, it can be accessed from the [[Calendar|Calendar and Events]] menu.
## Trivia
- One SkyBlock year lasts 372 SkyBlock days, with each month lasting 31 days exactly.
- There are exactly 124 [[Dark Auction|Dark Auctions]] and 124 [[Jacob's Farming Contest|Jacob's Farming Contests]] in one SkyBlock year.

# Fallen Stars
Aliases: Fallen Star
Categories: More info needed
[Fallen Stars]
Fallen Stars are randomly occurring events in the [[Dwarven Mines]] that spawn [[Star Sentry|Star Sentries]] and increase ore drops.
## Mechanics
The Fallen Star is an event that lasts roughly 5 minutes and 40 seconds. A Fallen Star will crash in one of the following locations, causing [[Star Sentry|Star Sentries]] to spawn around it.
- [[Royal Mines]]
- [[Far Reserve]]
- [[Goblin Burrows]]
- [[Upper Mines]]
- [[Rampart's Quarry]]
- [[Great Ice Wall]]
- [[Cliffside Veins]]
Fallen Stars increase [[Ores|Ore]] and ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] drops by an additive +2 per block mined.
While random, the odds of Fallen Stars spawning can be boosted by the perk [[Heart of the Mountain|Luck of the Cave]].
## Game Message
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Star Crash|✯ A Fallen Star has crashed at [location]! Nearby ore and Powder drops are amplified!|
## Trivia
- A special animation will be played prior to the star crash, starting near the [[Dwarven Mines|Purple Crystal]] in the [[Cliffside Veins]], where one of the orbiting stars will go off-course and travel towards its crash location.
- The [[2x Powder]] event works on the powder bonus.
- The ore drop increase is not multiplied by [[Mining Fortune]], [[Mining Fiesta]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Sky Mall's]] 5x [[Titanium]], being an additive bonus added after all drop multipliers [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.).
- [[Refined Mineral]] and [[Compact]] drops are tripled by the Fallen Star.
  - It remains unclear as to why the perk description does not reflect this.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jan 15, 2021|0.11|Added Fallen Star.|

# Floor Drops
Aliases: Floor Drop
Categories: More info needed
[Floor Drops]
Floor Drops can appear on the [[Moonglade Marsh]], [[Torrhus Canyon]] and [[Critter Safari]].
## Usage
### Moonglade Marsh
Floor Drops can appear on [[Grass|Grass Blocks]] around the [[Moonglade Marsh]]. Clicking on a Floor Drop grants one of the following rewards:
|Reward|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|1 [[Mudworm Shard]]|35|33.02% (1 in 3.03 rolls)|
|100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Fig Log]]|10|9.43% (1 in 10.6 rolls)|
|50 [[Fig Log]]|25|23.58% (1 in 4.24 rolls)|
|80 (1 stack plus 16) [[Mangrove Log]]|10|9.43% (1 in 10.6 rolls)|
|40 [[Mangrove Log]]|25|23.58% (1 in 4.24 rolls)|
|1 [[Invisibug Shard]]|1|0.94% (1 in 106.0 rolls)|
The rewards granted can be affected by [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[Hunting Fortune]].
### Torrhus Canyon
Floor Drops can appear on [[Grass|Grass Blocks]] around the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. Clicking on a Floor Drops grants one of the following rewards:
|Reward|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|32 [[Helix Log]]|100|20% (1 in 5 rolls)|
|64 (1 stack) [[Helix Log]]|50|10% (1 in 10 rolls)|
|250 [[Desert Whispers]]|100|20% (1 in 5 rolls)|
|500 [[Desert Whispers]]|50|10% (1 in 10 rolls)|
|1 [[Dung Beetle Shard]]|200|40% (1 in 2.5 rolls)|
The rewards granted can be affected by [[Foraging Fortune]] and [[Hunting Fortune]].
### Critter Safari
On the Critter Safari, Floor Drops have a chance to spawn in preset locations in all four biomes, with each biome having its own biome specific shard, and some biomes having biome specific items as well. During loot generation, 9 of the Forest Floor Drops become 3 [[Yogi Berry]], 3 [[Wriggleworm]], and 3 [[Bag of Seeds]], with the rest defaulting to either [[Hunting|Hunting XP]], [[Safari Essence]], [[Foxtrot Shard]], or [[Honeybug Shard]].[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.) Possible Rewards:
|Cavern|Forest|Haunted|Icy|
|-|-|-|-|
[1–16 Safari Essence]
[2,500-10,000 Hunting XP (Hunting)]
|1 [[Flitter Shard]]|1 [[Foxtrot Shard]]|1 [[Litterbug Shard]]|1 [[Strongarm Shard]]|
|1 [[Shyworm Shard]]|1 [[Honeybug Shard]]|1 [[Solsnatcher Shard]]|1 [[Polaris Shard]]|
|1 [[Purple Gem]]|1 [[Yogi Berry]]|1 [[Soothing Incense]]|1 [[Icebreaker]]|
|1 [[Lime Gem]]|1 [[Wriggleworm]]|1 [[Shining Coin]]||
|1 [[Orange Gem]]|1 [[Bag of Seeds]]|||

# Gemstone Powder
Categories: More info needed, Currencies
[Gemstone Powder]
Type: [[Currency]]
᠅ Gemstone Powder is a [[Currency]] used in the [[Heart of the Mountain]].
## Obtaining
Gemstone Powder can be obtained from various sources, as listed below:
- Mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]].
- Claiming [[Crystal Hollows]] [[Commissions]].
- Chests in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
- Completing a [[Crystal Nucleus]] run (at [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain X (10)]]).
- Accepting an offer from an [[Emissaries|Emissary]] on [[The Garden]].
- Killing a Diamond Goblin.
## Usage
- ᠅ Gemstone Powder is used to unlock [[Heart of the Mountain|Core of the Mountain]] Levels 4 to 7 and upgrade perks in [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tiers 4 to 7.
- Per 2,500 ᠅ Gemstone Powder until 600,000 ᠅ Gemstone Powder : [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 XP]] (Earn an exponentially decreasing amount of XP for powder above the cap until 20,000,000 ᠅ Gemstone Powder)
## Increasing Gemstone Powder Gain
### Heart of the Mountain
- Core of the Mountain Level 6: Grants +2 ᠅ Gemstone Powder per [[Gemstone]] mined.
- Sky Mall: Grants chance to gain +15% Powder while mining [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] for a [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]].
- Front Loaded: Grants 3x ᠅ Gemstone Powder for the first 2,500 [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] mined per real-life day.
- Daily Grind: Grants +500 ᠅ Gemstone Powder per Heart of the Mountain level for the first daily commission completed in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
- Daily Powder: Grants +500 ᠅ Gemstone Powder per Heart of the Mountain level for the first [[Gemstone]] mined per real-life day.
- Powder Buff: Increases Powder gain from all sources by +1-51%.
### Accessories
- [[Powder of the Few]]: Grants +5% ᠅ Gemstone Powder
- [[Powder of the Many]]: Grants +7.5% ᠅ Gemstone Powder
- [[Powder of the People]]: Grants +10% ᠅ Gemstone Powder
### Drills
- [[Ruby Drill TX-15]]: Grants +5% ᠅ Gemstone Powder while held.
- [[Gemstone Drill LT-522]]: Grants +10% ᠅ Gemstone Powder while held.
- [[Topaz Drill KGR-12]]: Grants +15% ᠅ Gemstone Powder while held.
- [[Jasper Drill X]]: Grants +20% ᠅ Gemstone Powder while held.
### Drill Parts
- [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]: Grants +1 level to unlocked Heart of the Mountain perks (+1% to Powder Buff).
- [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]]: Grants +20% Powder while a [[Drills|Drill]] with the part equipped is held.
### Pets
- [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Scatha Pet]]: Grants +0.2-20% ᠅ Gemstone Powder while equipped.
### Attributes
- [[Attributes|Atomized Crystals]]: Grants 1.01-1.1x ᠅ Gemstone Powder.
- [[Attributes|Echo of Atomized]]: Buffs the effect of [[Attributes|Atomized Crystals]] by 2-20%.
- [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]]: Buffs the effect of [[Attributes|Echo of Atomized]] by 5-50%.
### Events
- 2x Powder: Doubles all Powder gain for the duration of the event.
- +10% ᠅ Gemstone Powder while [[Weather|Rockfall]] is active.
### Others
- [[Doug]]'s Powder Hoarder Perk: Gain +2-6% ᠅ Gemstone Powder during the [[Mining Fiesta]].

## Trivia
- All sources of ᠅ Gemstone Powder buffs are additive, except the 2x Powder event.
- The maximum amount of unspent ᠅ Gemstone Powder a player may have is 2 billion exactly. This was reached for the first time by 2[[Ranks|[VIP]]] SamuriZombie on February 26th, 2026.
  - This increases to a maximum of spent and unspent 2,032,550,666 ᠅ Gemstone Powder, accounting for Core of the Mountain (1,400,000 ) and other [[Heart of the Mountain]] perks (31,150,666 ).
  - Resetting a [[Heart of the Mountain]] tree deletes the refunded overflowing powder.
  - On July 8th, 2026, the 0.26 Update forced a reset of [[Heart of the Mountain]] and [[Heart of the Forest]], inducing an integer overflow into the negatives, effectively wiping the progress. This was later fixed on October 15th, 2026.

# Glacite Powder
Categories: More info needed, Currencies
[Glacite Powder]
Type: [[Currency]]
᠅ Glacite Powder is a [[Currency]] used in the [[Heart of the Mountain]].
## Obtaining
Glacite Powder can be obtained from many different things, as listed below:
- Mining [[Glacite]].
- Claiming [[Glacite Tunnels]] [[Commissions]].
- Using the [[Fossil Excavator]].
- Looting [[Frozen Corpses]].
- Killing [[Glacite Maniacs]] and [[Littlefoot]].
- Donating [[Fossils|Fossil]] to [[Fossil Muncher]] (daily reward)
- Mining [[Glacite]] with Daily Powder Heart of the Mountain perk (daily reward)
- Claiming [[Glacite Tunnels]] [[Commissions]] with Daily Grind Heart of the Mountain perk (daily reward)
- Claiming [[Grandpa Wolf|Frozen Corpse Milestone]] from [[Grandpa Wolf]] (one time reward)
- Donating [[Fossils]] to [[Dr. Stone]] (one time reward per fossil type)
## Usage
- ᠅ Glacite Powder is used to unlock [[Heart of the Mountain|Core of the Mountain]] Levels 8 to 10 and upgrade perks in [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tiers 8 to 10.
- Per 2,500 ᠅ Glacite Powder until 600,000 ᠅ Glacite Powder : [[SkyBlock Levels|+1 XP]] (Earn an exponentially decreasing amount of XP for powder above the cap until 20,000,000 ᠅ Glacite Powder)
## Increasing Glacite Powder Gain
### Heart of the Mountain
- Core of the Mountain Level 8: Grants +3 ᠅ Glacite Powder per [[Glacite]] mined.
- Sky Mall: Grants chance to gain +15% Powder while mining for a [[Time Systems|SkyBlock Day]].
- Daily Grind: Grants +500 ᠅ Glacite Powder per Heart of the Mountain level for the first daily commission completed in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] or [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
- Daily Powder: Grants +500 ᠅ Glacite Powder per Heart of the Mountain level for the first [[Glacite]] mined per real-life day.
- Powder Buff: Increases Powder gain from all sources by +1-51%.
### Accessories
- [[Powder of the Many]]: Increases ᠅ Glacite Powder gain by +7.5%.
- [[Powder of the People]]: Increases ᠅ Glacite Powder gain by +10%.
### Drill Parts
- [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]: Grants +1 level to unlocked Heart of the Mountain perks (+1% to Powder Buff).
- [[Spicy Goblin Omelette]]: Grants +20% Powder while a [[Drills|Drill]] with the part equipped is held.
### Pets
- [[Glacite Golem Pet]]: Grants +0.2-20% ᠅ Glacite Powder while equipped.
### Attributes
- [[Attributes|Atomized Glacite]]: Grants 1.01-1.1x ᠅ Glacite Powder.
- [[Attributes|Echo of Atomized]]: Buffs the effect of [[Attributes|Atomized Glacite]] by 2-20%.
- [[Attributes|Echo of Echoes]]: Buffs the effect of [[Attributes|Echo of Atomized]] by 5-50%.
### Events
- 2x Powder: Doubles all Powder gain for the duration of the event.
- +10% ᠅ Glacite Powder while [[Weather|Snowstorm]] is active.
### Others
- [[Doug]]'s Powder Hoarder Perk: Gain +2-6% ᠅ Glacite Powder during the [[Mining Fiesta]].

# Heart of the Forest
Aliases: 250 Gifts, 250 Tree Gifts, Beekeeper, Center of the Forest, Daily Wishes, Free Trial, HOTF, Heart Of The Forest, Homing, HotF, Hotf, Luck of the Forest, Luck of the forest, Maniac Slicer, Maniac slicer
Categories: More info needed, Pages with UIs, Confirmations needed, Foraging
[Heart of the Forest]
Category: [[Skill Tree]]
The Heart of the Forest, commonly abbreviated as HotF, is a [[Skill Tree]] related to [[Foraging]]. Leveling it awards [[Token of the Forest|Tokens of the Forest]], which can be spent on various Perks found within the tree.
## Unlocking
The Heart of the Forest is unlocked by completing the [[Quests|Heart of the Forest]] quest from [[Hina]] in [[Tangleburg's Path]], which requires finishing talking to her and indirectly reaching [[Foraging|Foraging XII (12)]].
As players tier up their Heart of the Forest, they will gain [[Token of the Forest|Tokens of the Forest]], which can be used to unlock various perks. Some of these perks will take effect only on Public Islands with a special mining mechanism (alongside an infinite Mining Fatigue effect).
## UI
### Accessing
- The menu can be accessed :
  - via the /hotf command (anywhere)
  - via the [[Foraging]] menu (anywhere),
  - via the [[SkyBlock Menu]] (while on [[Galatea]], in other words at the [[Moonglade Marsh]] and [[Torrhus Canyon]]).
## Leveling
- Players can tier up their Heart of the Forest by gaining [[Heart of the Forest]] HotF EXP, which can be accumulated by:
  - opening [[Tree Gifts]] on [[Galatea]];
  - participating in a [[Starlyn Contest]]:
    - Depending on their respective Point Brackets, they grant 100-750 [[Heart of the Forest]] HotF EXP at the [[Moonglade Marsh]] and 150-900 [[Heart of the Forest]] HotF EXP on the [[Torrhus Canyon]].
    - The player can participate in both of their variants at the same time and reap their rewards, including [[Heart of the Forest]] HotF EXP.
    - The first 3 daily [[Starlyn Contest|Starlyn Contests]] award an additional +1200 [[Heart of the Forest]] HotF EXP. At HotF Level 8, this bonus is replaced with 1,200 [[Forest Whispers]] or [[Desert Whispers]] for [[Agatha]]'s or [[Miria]]'s contests.
- [[Heart of the Forest]] HotF EXP gains can be increased by 1%→10% from the [[Attributes|Galatea Training]] attribute ( [[Ember Shard]]).
- The Heart of the Forest can be reset. This reverts all perk purchases and reimburses all spent [[Forest Whispers]], [[Desert Whispers]] and [[Token of the Forest|Tokens of the Forest]].
  - Tier and Center of the Forest levels are kept; thus powder spent on upgrading Center of the Forest is not reimbursed.

## Axe Abilities
- Heart of the Forest Tiers 2 and 6 offer 2 and 1 Axe Abilities respectively.
- They can all be unlocked on the tree at once, but only one can be selected at a time.
- They are activated with Right-Click, and have either an instant effect or a period of effect.
- They have a cooldown, which starts immediately upon activation, not at the point of the ability's conclusion.
  - The player must wait for the cooldown to expire before being able to use any other Axe Ability.
  - The cooldown is preserved across lobbies. [Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- They are at Level 1 by default, and are upgradeable to Level 5[Confirm] by the following:
  - Level 1 of the Center of the Forest perk;
  - [More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)
- They do not apply on [[Huntaxes]].
- They do not apply at the [[Hub Island|Hub]] despite the existence of Oak Trees.
- Unlocked at Heart of the Forest Tier 2.
- Grants all Axes the Damage Boost ability, activated with Right-Click.
- The player's axe deals double [[Damage]] to creatures on Galatea for 10 seconds.

- Axe Throwing suffers no [[Sweep]] penalty for 10 seconds.

## Trivia
- While on [[Galatea]], players with Half Full enabled will have ⬓ next to their name; likewise, players with Half Empty enabled will have ⬒ next to their name.
- Despite what the menu suggests, perks from the Heart of the Forest do not require certain perks to be chosen. They can be unlocked if an adjacent perk is also unlocked.
- The Iron Lungs perk could be a reference to the video game and movie Iron Lung, as both involve diving deep underwater for exploration. This, in turn, also references the medical device of the same name.
- Despite being impossible to get every single perk in the Heart of the Forest, if one were to get all of them and max each one out (including Core of the Forest), it would cost:
  - 14,767,237 [[Forest Whispers]];
  - 52,672,500 [[Desert Whispers]];
  - or a combined amount of 67,439,737 Desert and Forest Whispers;
  - 35 [[Token of the Forest|Tokens of the Forest]].
