# Heart of the Mountain
Aliases: Arkenstone, Block Head, Blockhead, Core of the Mountain, Daily Grind, Daily Powder, Efficient Miner, Efficient miner, Front Loaded, Great Explorer, Great explorer, HOTM, Heart Of The Mountain, Heart of The Mountain, HotM, HotM Experience, Hotm, Hotm Exp, Hotm Xp, Hotm exp, Hotm xp, Lonesome Miner, Lonesome miner, Luck of the Cave, Luck of the cave, Maniac Miner, Maniac miner, Mineshaft Mayhem, Mining Ability, Mining Speed Boost, Mining ability, Mining speed boost, Peak of the Mountain, Pickaxe Ability, Pickaxe ability, Pickobulus, PotM, Powder Buff, Powder buff, Quick Forge, Quick forge, Sky Mall, Skymall, Tunnel Vision, Tunnel vision
Categories: More info needed, Confirmations needed, Pages with UIs, Mining, Quest Rewards, Pages with math equations
[Heart of the Mountain]
Category: [[Skill Tree]]
The Heart of the Mountain, commonly abbreviated as HotM, is a [[Skill Tree]] related to [[Mining]]. Leveling it awards perks and [[Token of the Mountain|Tokens of the Mountain]], which can be spent on various Perks found within the tree.
## Unlocking
The [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain is unlocked by completing the [[Quests|There are Dwarves?]] quest from [[Rhys]] in the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]], which requires giving him 15 of any enchanted [[Ores|ore]] and reaching [[Mining|Mining XII (12)]].
As players tier up their [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain, they will gain [[Token of the Mountain|Tokens of the Mountain]], which can be used to unlock various perks. Some of these perks will take effect only on Public Islands with a special mining mechanism (alongside an infinite Mining Fatigue effect).
## UI
- The tree layout above is not built for a specific purpose.
### Accessing
- The menu can be accessed :
  - via the /hotm command (anywhere)
  - via the [[Mining]] menu (anywhere),
  - via the [[SkyBlock Menu]] (while inside the [[Dwarven Mines]], [[Crystal Hollows]], [[Glacite Tunnels]] and [[Glacite Mineshafts]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]).
## Leveling
- Players can tier up their [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain by gaining [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP, which can be accumulated by:
  - completing [[Commissions]]:
    - from the [[Dwarven Mines]], each rewarding +100 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP at HotM Level 1, +200 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP at HotM Level 2 and +400 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP at HotM Level 3 and above
    - from the [[Crystal Hollows]], each rewarding +400 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP.
    - from the [[Glacite Tunnels]], each rewarding +750 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP.
    - The first 4 daily [[Commissions]] award an additional +900 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP. At HotM Level 10, this bonus is replaced with 1,600 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], 3,200 ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] or 4,000 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]], depending on the area.
  - participating in various active [[Dwarven Mines|Mining Events]].
  - completing [[Crystal Nucleus]] Runs, each rewarding +800 [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP.
- [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP gains from [[Commissions]] can be increased by 1%→10% from the [[Attributes|Mountain Climber]] attribute ( [[Troglobyte Shard]]).
- All [[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP can be increased by 1%→10% from the [[Attributes|Dwarven Training]] attribute ( [[Treasure Hoarder Shard]]).
- The [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain can be reset. This reverts all perk purchases and reimburses all spent ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]], ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] and [[Token of the Mountain|Tokens of the Mountain]].
  - Tier and Core of the Mountain levels are kept; thus powder spent on upgrading Core of the Mountain is not reimbursed.
|Tier|[[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP Required To Upgrade|[[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM EXP Required Cumulative|TotM (Tokens of the Mountain) Given From Upgrade|TotM (Tokens of the Mountain) Given Cumulative|Forge ([[The Forge]]) Slots|SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|Other Rewards|

## Pickaxe Abilities
[[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain Tiers 2, 6 and 10 offer 2 Pickaxe Abilities each, for a total of 6.
- They can all be unlocked on the tree at once, but only one can be selected at a time.
- They are activated with Right-Click, and have either an instant effect or a period of effect.
- Their name is misleading as they can be used while holding a [[Tools|Mining Tool]]: a [[Drills|Drill]], a [[Pickaxes|Pickaxe]] or a [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
### List
- Unlocked at Heart of the Mountain Tier 2.
- Grants all mining tools the Mining Speed Boost ability, activated with Right-Click.
- Grants +x% [[Mining Speed]] for y seconds.
|Level|Effect (x%)|Duration (y)|Cooldown|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|200|10 seconds|120 seconds|
|2|250|15 seconds|120 seconds|
|3|300|20 seconds|120 seconds|
- Not all sources [[Mining Speed]] are accounted for in the calculation of Mining Speed Boost. Only those appearing in the /stats breakdown are, while other sources, often restricted to a type of block, are not.
  ✔ [[Mining Speed]] from [[Flowstate]], from the [[Reforging|Heated]] ( [[Scorched Topaz]]) reforge bonus or the [[Power Scrolls|Amber Power Scroll]] ✕ [[Mining Speed]] from the [[Jasper Drill X]] on [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], from the Strong Arm perk on [[Dwarven Metals]], from the [[Mithril Golem Pet]]'s Mithril Affinity perk on [[Mithril]].
- Unlocked at Heart of the Mountain Tier 2.
- Grants all mining tools the Pickobolus ability, activated with Right-Click.
- Throws the [[Tools|Mining Tool]] to create an explosion on impact, mining ores within a 3 blocks radius.
|Level|Radius|Cooldown|
|-|-|-|
|1|3 blocks|60 seconds|
|2|3 blocks|50 seconds|
|3|3 blocks|40 seconds|
- [[Mining Spread]] and [[Gemstone Spread]] do not apply on "ores" broken from this ability. [[Machine Fuel|Fuel]] is not depleted.
- In this context, "Ores" is a generic term that covers [[Blocks]], [[Ores]], [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] and [[Dwarven Metals]].
- Unlocked at Heart of the Mountain Tier 6.
- Grants all mining tools the Tunnel Vision ability, activated with Right-Click.
- Increases the respective chances for the player to trigger the following rare occurences by +x1% for 30 seconds:
  - [[Golden Goblin|Golden Goblins]] only
  - [[Fallen Stars]]
  - [[Powder Ghast|Powder Ghasts]]
  - [[Stoneworm]] [[Scatha]] [[Worm (disambiguation)|Worms]].
  - [[Glacite Mineshafts]]
- Final Chance=Base Chance×(1+x1100+x2100)
  - x2 corresponds to the [[Heart of the Mountain|Luck of the Cave]] perk's level. If the event type exists in the [[Glacite Tunnels]] (i.e. [[Golden Goblin|Golden Goblins]]), it is then applied. Else, it is ignored.
  - Unaffected by [[Tracking]].
|Level|Effect (x1%)|Duration|Cooldown|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|30|30 seconds|120 seconds|
|2|40|30 seconds|110 seconds|
|3|50|30 seconds|110 seconds|
- Unlocked at Heart of the Mountain Tier 6.
- Grants all mining tools the Maniac Miner ability, activated with Right-Click.
- Grants +1 [[Breaking Power]] and a stack of +x [[Mining Fortune]] per block broken for y seconds. Caps at a total of 500 [[Mining Fortune]]. Each block broken consumes 20 [[Mana]].
|Level|Effect (x)|Duration (y)|Cooldown|
|-|-|-|-|
|1|5 [[Mining Fortune]]|25 seconds|120 seconds|
|2|10 [[Mining Fortune]]|30 seconds|120 seconds|
|3|15 [[Mining Fortune]]|35 seconds|120 seconds|
- The [[Intelligence|Mana Cost]] is affected by [[Ultimate Wise]] and by the [[Attributes|Breeze]] attribute ( [[Wither Spectre Shard]]).
- Maniac Miner's duration is preserved upon swapping away from a [[Drills|Drill]] equipped with a [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]], though the stack of [[Mining Fortune]] is not.
- Unlocked at Heart of the Mountain Tier 10.
- Grants all mining tools the Gemstone Infusion ability, activated with Right-Click.
- Increases the effectiveness of every Gemstone in the Drill's Gemstone Slots by 100% for x seconds.
|Level|Duration (x)|Cooldown|
|-|-|-|
|1|20 seconds|120 seconds|
|2|25 seconds|120 seconds|
|3|30 seconds|120 seconds|
- The stats are granted as player [[Stats]] until the ability expires, not on the mining tool itself.
- This ability can be used on the [[Gemstone Gauntlet]].
- Unlocked at Heart of the Mountain Tier 10.
- Grants all mining tools the Sheer Force ability, activated with Right-Click.
- Grants 200 [[Mining Spread]] for x seconds.
|Level|Duration (x)|Cooldown|
|-|-|-|
|1|20 seconds|120 seconds|
|2|25 seconds|120 seconds|
|3|30 seconds|120 seconds|
### Levels Increases
They are at Level 1 by default, and are upgradeable up to Level 3 from the following sources:
|Level Increase|Type of Source|Source|
|-|-|-|
|+1|[[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM perk|Level 2 of the Core of the Mountain|
|+1|Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])|[[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]|
### Cooldown Reductions
They have a cooldown, which starts immediately upon activation, not at the point of the ability's conclusion.
- The player must wait for the cooldown to expire before being able to use any other Pickaxe Ability.
- The cooldown is preserved across lobbies, and set to zero when entering a [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
- The cooldown can be reduced from the following sources, stacking multiplicatively:
|Cooldown Reduction|Type of Source|Source|Condition|
|-|-|-|-|
|-1% → -10%|Attribute ([[Attributes]])|Pickaxe Cooldown ([[Attributes]]) ( [[Abyssal Miner Shard]])||
|-20%|[[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM perk|Sky Mall, at a chance of 22.73% (1 in 4.4 SkyBlock Days ([[Time Systems]]))|[[Mining Islands]]|
|-25%|[[Heart of the Mountain]] HotM perk|Mineshaft Mayhem, at a chance of 20% (1 in 5 [[Glacite Mineshafts]])|[[Glacite Mineshafts]]|
|-2%|Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])|[[Mithril-Infused Fuel Tank]]||
|-4%|Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])|[[Titanium-Infused Fuel Tank]]||
|-6%|Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])|[[Gemstone Fuel Tank]]||
|-10%|Drill Part ([[Drill Parts]])|[[Perfectly-Cut Fuel Tank]]||

  - Unaffected by [[Tracking]].

## Bugs
- With an active [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]] ability and using a [[Tools|Mining Tool]] that would otherwise have a [[Breaking Power]] too low to break a given block, [[Gemstone Spread]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; Not sure for gemstone spread; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)] and [[Mining Spread]] do not apply.
- The [[Intelligence|Mana Cost]] of Maniac Miner Pickaxe Ability is consumed even after reaching the [[Mining Fortune]] cap.
- At CotM (Core of the Mountain) level 2, using a [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] can bypass the cap of the Maniac Miner level 3 ability by 10 [[Mining Fortune]], from 500 to 510 .
  At level 3 of Maniac Miner, each block broken grants +15 [[Mining Fortune]] . The check for the cap is run after the incrementation. In practice, this gives: +15 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → +30 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → […] → +480 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → +495 [[Mining Fortune]] → PASS → +510 [[Mining Fortune]] → STOP.
## Trivia
- Reaching [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain Tier 5 will reward the player with the [[Achievements|"Core of the Mountain" Achievement]].
- [[2x Powder]] events also double [[Powder (disambiguation)|Powder]] obtained from the Daily Powder perk though the message does not reflect this. This is now affected by Powder Buff.
- All [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain Tier 7 perks cost the same amount of ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]].
- Despite being impossible to get every single perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain, if one were to get all of them and max each one out (including Core of the Mountain), it would cost :
  - 12,808,220 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]];
  - 32,550,666 ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]];
  - 37,479,533 ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]];
  - or a combined amount of 82,838,419 ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]] and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]];
  - 45 Tokens of the Mountain.
- The [[Heart of the Mountain]] Heart of the Mountain is likely a reference to the Arkenstone from J.R.R. Tolkien's The Hobbit Chapter 4 (Over Hill and Under Hill).
  - The Arkenstone is the biggest and rarest gemstone in the Lonely Mountain, and valued most by the Dwarves.
- Lonesome Miner's perk also affects [[Legion]], [[Superior Dragon Armor]], [[Ender Dragon Pet|Ender Dragon Pets]] and any other items that affects the buffed stats.
- The [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] [[Crow Pet]]'s Insightful perk does not apply on [[Heart of the Mountain|Maniac Miner]].
- The Maniac Miner perk is based on a team upgrade in Hypixel Bed Wars, which grants [[Haste Potion|Haste I]] and [[Haste Potion|Haste II]].

The Mining Speed II and Mining Fortune II were renamed to Speedy Mineman and Fortunate Mineman respectively and nerfed. The Orbiter perk was removed; it increased the chance to gain additional experience orbs upon mining ores. The Mining Madness perk was removed; it granted +50 [[Mining Speed]] and +50 [[Mining Fortune]]. The Crystallized perk was removed; it granted 200 [[Mining Speed]] and a 200% chance to deal more damage to a [[Star Sentry]] if you were near [[Fallen Stars]]. The Goblin Killer perk was removed; it granted ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] depending on the [[Goblin]] type killed. The Star Powder perk was removed; it granted extra ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] when mining near [[Fallen Stars]]. The Vein Seeker ability was removed; it pointed your view in the direction of the nearest vein and granted [[Mining Spread]].|

# Honeyhives
Aliases: Honeyhive
Categories: More info needed
[Honeyhives]
Honeyhives are structures in the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. They are the primary source of [[Honeycomb]].
## Overview
Honeyhives are found all across the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. They can be interacted with to obtain [[Honeycomb]]. Additionally, the player has a chance to obtain [[Enchanted Honeycomb]], [[Coins]], and a random bee-related [[Attributes|Shard]]. The [[Honeybuzz Shard]]'s [[Attributes|Honey Haul]] attribute grants a chance to double the amount of [[Honeycomb]] obtained.
A [[Honeybuzz]] has a 50% (1 in 2) chance to spawn upon looting a Honeyhive. This yields the following message:
A Honeybuzz appeared, angry at you for stealing its honey!
Looting a Honeyhive yields the following message:
You stick your hand into the honeyhive and feel around...
HIVE! You found Honeycome x# and <extra loot>!
Looting a Honeyhive causes it to go on cooldown for 60m. The [[Queen Bee Shard]]'s [[Attributes|Honey Refill]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants a chance to instantly refill the Honeyhive.
### Possible Loot
Honeyhives are guaranteed to drop 16–64 (1 stack) [[Honeycomb|Honeycombs]] and another item from the pool:
|Slot|Name|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|-|
|[[Coins]] x1750|1,000–2,500 [[Coins]]|100|20.83% (1 in 4.8 rolls)|

### Beekeeper Perk
Having [[Heart of the Forest|Beekeeper]] unlocked in the [[Heart of the Forest]] allows the player to gain a random effect that benefits Honeyhives.
|Possible Effect|Weight|Chance|
|-|-|-|
|[[Honeyhives]] refill 20% faster.|[More Info Needed] (More infomation needed; When this info is added, remove this.)|25 (1 in 4 rolls)|
|Trees lathered with [[Honeycomb]] attract [[Critters]] 20% faster.|(same as above)|25 (1 in 4 rolls)|
|Gain 2x [[Honeycomb]] from [[Honeyhives]] and lathering Trees with Honeycomb ([[Pots of Honeycomb]]).|(same as above)|25 (1 in 4 rolls)|
|Trees lathered with Honeycomb ([[Pots of Honeycomb]]) have a 25% chance to attract a second Critter ([[Critters]]).|(same as above)|25 (1 in 4 rolls)|
